import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class UserManagementScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const UserManagementScreen({super.key});

  void _editUserDialog(BuildContext context, String userId, Map<String, dynamic> data, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final nameController = TextEditingController(text: data['name']);
    String selectedRole = data['role'] ?? 'patient';
    final adminService = AdminService();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
          title: Text(l10n.editUserTitle),
          content: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextField(
                controller: nameController,
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.nameLabel),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                initialValue: selectedRole,
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.roleLabel),
                items: [
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: 'patient', child: Text(l10n.rolePatient)),
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: 'doctor', child: Text(l10n.roleDoctor)),
                  DropdownMenuItem(value: 'admin', child: Text(l10n.roleAdmin)),
                ],
                onChanged: (value) => setDialogState(() => selectedRole = value!),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          actionsPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
          actions: [
            TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: Text(l10n.cancel)),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () async {
                await adminService.updateUser(userId, {
                  'name': nameController.text.trim(),
                  'role': selectedRole,
                });
                await adminService.writeLog(
                  entity: 'user',
                  entityId: userId,
                  action: 'update',
                  performedBy: AuthService().currentUser!.uid,
                );
                if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
              },
              child: Text(l10n.save),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _confirmDelete(BuildContext context, String userId, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final adminService = AdminService();
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: Text(l10n.deleteUserTitle),
        content: Text(l10n.deleteUserBody),
        actionsPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: Text(l10n.cancel)),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent),
            onPressed: () async {
              await adminService.deleteUser(userId);
              await adminService.writeLog(
                entity: 'user',
                entityId: userId,
                action: 'delete',
                performedBy: AuthService().currentUser!.uid,
              );
              if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
            },
            child: Text(l10n.delete),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AdminService adminService = AdminService();
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.manageUsersTitle)),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: adminService.getAllUsers(),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return Center(child: Text(l10n.noUsersFound, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500)));
          }

          final users = snapshot.data!.docs;

          return ListView.builder(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            itemCount: users.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final doc = users[index];
              final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              final role = data['role'] ?? 'unknown';
              final roleInfo = _roleInfo(role, l10n);

              return Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      CircleAvatar(
                        backgroundColor: roleInfo.color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                        child: Icon(Icons.person_rounded, color: roleInfo.color),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(data['name'] ?? l10n.unnamed, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 14.5)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 2),
                            Text(data['email'] ?? '', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 6),
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 3),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: roleInfo.color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              ),
                              child: Text(roleInfo.label,
                                  style: TextStyle(color: roleInfo.color, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined, color: Colors.grey, size: 20),
                        onPressed: () => _editUserDialog(context, doc.id, data, l10n),
                      ),
                      IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline_rounded, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 20),
                        onPressed: () => _confirmDelete(context, doc.id, l10n),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  ({Color color, String label}) _roleInfo(String role, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (role) {
      case 'doctor':
        return (color: AppTheme.primaryColor, label: l10n.roleDoctor);
      case 'admin':
        return (color: const Color(0xFF6366F1), label: l10n.roleAdmin);
      default:
        return (color: const Color(0xFF0EA5E9), label: l10n.rolePatient);
    }
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\auth\firebase_auth.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class AuthService {
  final FirebaseAuth _auth = FirebaseAuth.instance;
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // Current logged-in user
  User? get currentUser => _auth.currentUser;

  // Sign up a new user (patient or doctor). Returns a
  // FirebaseAuthException *code* (e.g. 'email-already-in-use') rather
  // than e.message on failure, so the UI can show a localized message
  // via utils/auth_error_translator.dart — e.message is English-only
  // and can't be translated.
  Future<String?> signUp({
    required String name,
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String role, // "patient" or "doctor"
  }) async {
    try {
      UserCredential result = await _auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
        email: email,
        password: password,
      );

      // Save extra user info in Firestore
      await _firestore.collection('users').doc(result.user!.uid).set({
        'userId': result.user!.uid,
        'name': name,
        'email': email,
        'role': role,
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });

      return null; // null means success
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      return e.code;
    }
  }

  // Log in existing user. Returns a FirebaseAuthException code on
  // failure — see note on signUp above.
  Future<String?> logIn({
    required String email,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    try {
      await _auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email: email, password: password);
      return null; // success
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      return e.code;
    }
  }

  // Get the role of the currently logged-in user
  Future<String?> getUserRole(String uid) async {
    DocumentSnapshot doc = await _firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    if (doc.exists) {
      return doc.get('role') as String;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Log out
  Future<void> signOut() async {
    await _auth.signOut();
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\auth\login_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import 'signup_screen.dart';
import '../patient/patient_home_screen.dart';
import '../doctor/doctor_home_screen.dart';
import '../admin/admin_home_screen.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import '../../utils/auth_error_translator.dart';

class LoginScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginScreen> createState() => _LoginScreenState();
}

class _LoginScreenState extends State<LoginScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final AuthService _authService = AuthService();

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  String? _errorMessage;

  Future<void> _handleLogin() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _errorMessage = null;
    });

    final error = await _authService.logIn(
      email: _emailController.text.trim(),
      password: _passwordController.text.trim(),
    );

    if (error != null) {
      setState(() {
        _isLoading = false;
        _errorMessage = authErrorMessage(l10n, error, isSignup: false);
      });
      return;
    }

    final uid = _authService.currentUser!.uid;
    final role = await _authService.getUserRole(uid);

    setState(() => _isLoading = false);

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (role == 'patient') {
      Navigator.pushReplacement(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const PatientHomeScreen()));
    } else if (role == 'doctor') {
      Navigator.pushReplacement(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const DoctorHomeScreen()));
    } else if (role == 'admin') {
      Navigator.pushReplacement(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AdminHomeScreen()));
    } else {
      setState(() => _errorMessage = l10n.unknownRole);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Center(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 28),
            child: Form(
              key: _formKey,
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Container(
                    height: 76,
                    width: 76,
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.health_and_safety_rounded, size: 40, color: AppTheme.primaryColor),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  Text(l10n.welcomeBack,
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFF1E293B))),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Text(l10n.loginSubtitle,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: Colors.grey.shade600)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 32),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _emailController,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      labelText: l10n.emailLabel,
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.mail_outline),
                    ),
                    validator: (value) {
                      if (value == null || value.isEmpty) return l10n.enterEmail;
                      if (!value.contains('@')) return l10n.enterValidEmail;
                      return null;
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _passwordController,
                    obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      labelText: l10n.passwordLabel,
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                      suffixIcon: IconButton(
                        icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined),
                        onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
                      ),
                    ),
                    validator: (value) {
                      if (value == null || value.length < 6) return l10n.passwordMinLength;
                      return null;
                    },
                  ),
                  if (_errorMessage != null) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.red.shade50,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 18),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          Expanded(child: Text(_errorMessage!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent, fontSize: 13))),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                  const SizedBox(height: 22),
                  ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleLogin,
                    child: _isLoading
                        ? const SizedBox(
                      height: 20, width: 20,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white),
                    )
                        : Text(l10n.logIn),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () {
                      Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SignupScreen()));
                    },
                    child: Text(l10n.noAccountSignUp),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\auth\signup_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import '../../utils/auth_error_translator.dart';

class SignupScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SignupScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SignupScreen> createState() => _SignupScreenState();
}

class _SignupScreenState extends State<SignupScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final AuthService _authService = AuthService();

  String _selectedRole = 'patient';
  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  String? _errorMessage;

  Future<void> _handleSignup() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    setState(() {
      _isLoading = true;
      _errorMessage = null;
    });

    final error = await _authService.signUp(
      name: _nameController.text.trim(),
      email: _emailController.text.trim(),
      password: _passwordController.text.trim(),
      role: _selectedRole,
    );

    setState(() => _isLoading = false);

    if (error != null) {
      setState(() => _errorMessage = authErrorMessage(l10n, error, isSignup: true));
      return;
    }

    if (!mounted) return;
    Navigator.pop(context);
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(l10n.accountCreated)),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.createAccount)),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(28),
          child: Form(
            key: _formKey,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(l10n.joinNeuroBridge,
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Colors.grey.shade900)),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(l10n.signupSubtitle,
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: Colors.grey.shade600)),
                const SizedBox(height: 28),
                TextFormField(
                  controller: _nameController,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.fullNameLabel, prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.person_outline)),
                  validator: (value) => (value == null || value.isEmpty) ? l10n.enterName : null,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                TextFormField(
                  controller: _emailController,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.emailLabel, prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.mail_outline)),
                  validator: (value) {
                    if (value == null || value.isEmpty) return l10n.enterEmail;
                    if (!value.contains('@')) return l10n.enterValidEmail;
                    return null;
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                TextFormField(
                  controller: _passwordController,
                  obscureText: _obscurePassword,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: l10n.passwordLabel,
                    prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                    suffixIcon: IconButton(
                      icon: Icon(_obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined),
                      onPressed: () => setState(() => _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword),
                    ),
                  ),
                  validator: (value) {
                    if (value == null || value.length < 6) return l10n.passwordMinLength;
                    return null;
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 22),
                Text(l10n.iAmA, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: Colors.grey.shade800)),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: _RoleCard(
                        label: l10n.rolePatient,
                        icon: Icons.person,
                        selected: _selectedRole == 'patient',
                        onTap: () => setState(() => _selectedRole = 'patient'),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: _RoleCard(
                        label: l10n.roleDoctor,
                        icon: Icons.medical_services,
                        selected: _selectedRole == 'doctor',
                        onTap: () => setState(() => _selectedRole = 'doctor'),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                if (_errorMessage != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red.shade50, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        const Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 18),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(child: Text(_errorMessage!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent, fontSize: 13))),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                ElevatedButton(
                  onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleSignup,
                  child: _isLoading
                      ? const SizedBox(
                    height: 20, width: 20,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white),
                  )
                      : Text(l10n.signUp),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _RoleCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _RoleCard({required this.label, required this.icon, required this.selected, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 18),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : Colors.grey.shade100,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          border: Border.all(color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.transparent, width: 1.6),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade500),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text(label,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade600,
                )),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\chat\chat_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../services/chat_service.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class ChatScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String otherUserId;
  final String otherUserName;

  const ChatScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.otherUserId,
    required this.otherUserName,
  });

  @override
  State<ChatScreen> createState() => _ChatScreenState();
}

class _ChatScreenState extends State<ChatScreen> {
  final ChatService _chatService = ChatService();
  final TextEditingController _messageController = TextEditingController();
  late final String _myId;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _myId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;
    // Ask for AI tone-analysis consent once, after the first frame so
    // `context` is ready for showDialog.
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _maybeAskForSentimentConsent());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _messageController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _maybeAskForSentimentConsent({bool force = false}) async {
    if (!force) {
      final existing = await _chatService.getSentimentConsent(_myId);
      if (existing != null) return; // already asked before
    }
    if (!mounted) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    final agreed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(l10n.aiToneAnalysisTitle),
        content: Text(l10n.aiToneAnalysisBody),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
            child: Text(l10n.noThanks),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
            child: Text(l10n.iAgree),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    await _chatService.setSentimentConsent(_myId, agreed ?? false);
  }

  void _sendMessage() {
    final text = _messageController.text.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty) return;

    _chatService.sendMessage(
      senderId: _myId,
      receiverId: widget.otherUserId,
      content: text,
    );
    _messageController.clear();
  }

  Future<void> _confirmDeleteMessage(String messageId) async {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final confirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(l10n.deleteMessageTitle),
        content: Text(l10n.deleteMessageBody),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
            child: Text(l10n.cancel),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
            child: Text(l10n.delete, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (confirm == true) {
      await _chatService.deleteMessage(
        userId1: _myId,
        userId2: widget.otherUserId,
        messageId: messageId,
      );
    }
  }

  Widget? _buildSentimentBadge(String? sentiment, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    if (sentiment == null) return null;
    Color color;
    IconData icon;
    String label;
    switch (sentiment) {
      case 'Distressed':
        color = Colors.red;
        icon = Icons.sentiment_very_dissatisfied;
        label = l10n.sentimentDistressed;
        break;
      case 'Calm':
        color = Colors.green;
        icon = Icons.sentiment_satisfied_alt;
        label = l10n.sentimentCalm;
        break;
      case 'Neutral':
      default:
        color = Colors.grey;
        icon = Icons.sentiment_neutral;
        label = l10n.sentimentNeutral;
    }
    return Tooltip(
      message: label,
      child: Icon(icon, size: 15, color: color),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildConsentBanner(AppLocalizations l10n) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => _maybeAskForSentimentConsent(force: true),
      child: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        color: AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.06),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 7),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.privacy_tip_outlined, size: 14, color: Colors.blueGrey),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                l10n.consentBannerText,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.blueGrey),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMessageBubble(QueryDocumentSnapshot doc, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final isMe = data['senderId'] == _myId;
    final sentiment = data['sentiment'] as String?;
    // Only show the tone indicator on the OTHER person's
    // messages — e.g. the doctor sees it on the patient's
    // bubbles, not their own.
    final badge = !isMe ? _buildSentimentBadge(sentiment, l10n) : null;

    return Align(
      alignment: isMe ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: GestureDetector(
        onLongPress: isMe ? () => _confirmDeleteMessage(doc.id) : null,
        child: Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
          constraints: BoxConstraints(
            maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.75,
          ),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: isMe ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade200,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  data['content'] as String? ?? '',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: isMe ? Colors.white : Colors.black87,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              if (badge != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                badge,
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMessageInput(AppLocalizations l10n) {
    return SafeArea(
      top: false,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white,
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
              blurRadius: 4,
              offset: const Offset(0, -1),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: TextField(
                controller: _messageController,
                minLines: 1,
                maxLines: 4,
                textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: l10n.typeMessageHint,
                  filled: true,
                  fillColor: Colors.grey.shade100,
                  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                    borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                  ),
                ),
                onSubmitted: (_) => _sendMessage(),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            IconButton(
              onPressed: _sendMessage,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.send),
              color: AppTheme.primaryColor,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        titleSpacing: 0,
        title: Row(
          children: [
            CircleAvatar(
              backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              child: const Icon(Icons.person, color: AppTheme.primaryColor, size: 20),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text(widget.otherUserName, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          _buildConsentBanner(l10n),
          Expanded(
            child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
              stream: _chatService.getMessages(_myId, widget.otherUserId),
              builder: (context, snapshot) {
                if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                  return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                }
                if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
                  return Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Icon(Icons.waving_hand_rounded, size: 40, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                        const SizedBox(height: 10),
                        Text(l10n.sayHello, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  );
                }

                final messages = snapshot.data!.docs;

                return ListView.builder(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  itemCount: messages.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    return _buildMessageBubble(messages[index], l10n);
                  },
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
          _buildMessageInput(l10n),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\doctor\ai_summary_card.dart
################################################################################

// lib/screens/doctor/ai_summary_card.dart
//
// FR-D-02: Doctor views the AI-generated summary of a patient's
// questionnaire, along with an urgency triage badge and a trend/progress
// note when the patient has submitted more than once. If the patient
// wrote their complaint in Urdu/Roman Urdu, the English translation is
// shown alongside the original. Drop this into the patient profile
// screen on the doctor side. Firestore security rules (separate task)
// should ensure only the assigned doctor can read a given patient's
// responses.

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class AiSummaryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String patientId;

  const AiSummaryCard({super.key, required this.patientId});

  Color _urgencyColor(String urgency) {
    switch (urgency) {
      case 'Urgent':
        return Colors.red;
      case 'Moderate':
        return Colors.orange;
      case 'Routine':
      default:
        return Colors.green;
    }
  }

  String _urgencyLabel(String urgency, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (urgency) {
      case 'Urgent':
        return l10n.urgencyUrgent;
      case 'Moderate':
        return l10n.urgencyModerate;
      case 'Routine':
      default:
        return l10n.urgencyRoutine;
    }
  }

  Widget _buildUrgencyBadge(String urgency, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final color = _urgencyColor(urgency);
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: color),
      ),
      child: Text(
        _urgencyLabel(urgency, l10n),
        style: TextStyle(color: color, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final query = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('questionnaireResponses')
        .where('patientId', isEqualTo: patientId)
        .orderBy('analyzedAt', descending: true)
        .limit(1);

    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: query.snapshots(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
          );
        }
        if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Text(l10n.noQuestionnaireYet),
          );
        }

        final doc = snapshot.data!.docs.first;
        final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final complaint = data['chiefComplaint'] ?? '';
        // Older records (submitted before translation support was added)
        // won't have this field, so fall back to the original complaint.
        final translatedComplaint = (data['translatedComplaint'] as String?) ?? complaint;
        final wasTranslated = translatedComplaint.trim() != complaint.toString().trim();
        final summary = data['analysisSummary'] ?? '';
        final urgency = (data['urgency'] as String?) ?? 'Routine';
        final progressNote = data['progressNote'] as String?;

        return Card(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.smart_toy_outlined, size: 20),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(l10n.aiPreAppointmentSummary,
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ),
                    _buildUrgencyBadge(urgency, l10n),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(l10n.chiefComplaintLabel(translatedComplaint),
                    style: const TextStyle(fontStyle: FontStyle.italic)),
                if (wasTranslated) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.translate, size: 14, color: Colors.grey),
                      const SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          l10n.translatedFromOriginal(complaint.toString()),
                          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.grey),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(summary),
                if (progressNote != null && progressNote.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.06),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.blue.withOpacity(0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        const Icon(Icons.trending_up, size: 18, color: Colors.blue),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            progressNote,
                            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\doctor\chat_sentiment_summary_card.dart
################################################################################

// lib/screens/doctor/chat_sentiment_summary_card.dart
//
// Doctor-facing card that summarizes the tone of a patient's chat messages
// over time (Distressed / Neutral / Calm), plus a simple trend read
// (recent messages vs earlier ones). Reuses the same `sentiment` field
// chat_service.dart already writes onto each message — this just
// aggregates it. Drop it into the patient profile screen next to
// AiSummaryCard.
//
// Only messages the patient has consented to (chatSentimentConsent) will
// ever carry a `sentiment` value, so if the patient never opted in, or
// hasn't sent enough messages yet, this shows a "not enough data" state
// instead of guessing.

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../services/chat_service.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class ChatSentimentSummaryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String patientId;
  final String doctorId;

  /// Minimum number of tagged messages before we're willing to show a
  /// trend/summary at all — a couple of messages isn't a "trend".
  static const int _minMessagesForSummary = 3;

  const ChatSentimentSummaryCard({
    super.key,
    required this.patientId,
    required this.doctorId,
  });

  Color _sentimentColor(String sentiment) {
    switch (sentiment) {
      case 'Distressed':
        return Colors.red;
      case 'Calm':
        return Colors.green;
      case 'Neutral':
      default:
        return Colors.grey;
    }
  }

  IconData _sentimentIcon(String sentiment) {
    switch (sentiment) {
      case 'Distressed':
        return Icons.sentiment_very_dissatisfied;
      case 'Calm':
        return Icons.sentiment_satisfied_alt;
      case 'Neutral':
      default:
        return Icons.sentiment_neutral;
    }
  }

  String _sentimentLabel(String sentiment, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (sentiment) {
      case 'Distressed':
        return l10n.sentimentDistressed;
      case 'Calm':
        return l10n.sentimentCalm;
      case 'Neutral':
      default:
        return l10n.sentimentNeutral;
    }
  }

  // Distressed=0, Neutral=1, Calm=2 — just for comparing "earlier" vs
  // "recent" average tone to describe a trend in plain language.
  int _score(String sentiment) {
    switch (sentiment) {
      case 'Distressed':
        return 0;
      case 'Calm':
        return 2;
      default:
        return 1;
    }
  }

  ({String label, IconData icon, Color color}) _trendFrom(List<String> sentiments, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final mid = sentiments.length ~/ 2;
    final earlier = sentiments.sublist(0, mid);
    final recent = sentiments.sublist(mid);
    final earlierAvg = earlier.map(_score).reduce((a, b) => a + b) / earlier.length;
    final recentAvg = recent.map(_score).reduce((a, b) => a + b) / recent.length;
    final delta = recentAvg - earlierAvg;

    if (delta > 0.25) {
      return (label: l10n.trendCalmer, icon: Icons.trending_up, color: Colors.green);
    } else if (delta < -0.25) {
      return (label: l10n.trendDistressed, icon: Icons.trending_down, color: Colors.red);
    }
    return (label: l10n.trendStable, icon: Icons.trending_flat, color: Colors.blueGrey);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final chatService = ChatService();
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: chatService.getMessages(patientId, doctorId),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
          );
        }

        // Only the patient's own messages that were actually tagged
        // (i.e. consent was given at send time) count toward this.
        final sentiments = (snapshot.data?.docs ?? [])
            .map((d) => d.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)
            .where((data) => data['senderId'] == patientId && data['sentiment'] != null)
            .map((data) => data['sentiment'] as String)
            .toList();

        if (sentiments.length < _minMessagesForSummary) {
          return Card(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 18, color: Colors.grey),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      l10n.notEnoughToneData,
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.grey),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        }

        final counts = <String, int>{'Distressed': 0, 'Neutral': 0, 'Calm': 0};
        for (final s in sentiments) {
          counts[s] = (counts[s] ?? 0) + 1;
        }
        final total = sentiments.length;
        final dominant = counts.entries.reduce((a, b) => a.value >= b.value ? a : b).key;
        final trend = _trendFrom(sentiments, l10n);

        return Card(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.forum_outlined, size: 20),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        l10n.chatToneSummaryTitle,
                        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Icon(_sentimentIcon(dominant), color: _sentimentColor(dominant), size: 18),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      _sentimentLabel(dominant, l10n),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: _sentimentColor(dominant),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 12,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                // Segmented proportion bar
                ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  child: SizedBox(
                    height: 10,
                    child: Row(
                      children: ['Distressed', 'Neutral', 'Calm'].map((key) {
                        final n = counts[key] ?? 0;
                        if (n == 0) return const SizedBox.shrink();
                        return Expanded(
                          flex: n,
                          child: Container(color: _sentimentColor(key)),
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 14,
                  runSpacing: 4,
                  children: ['Distressed', 'Neutral', 'Calm'].map((key) {
                    final n = counts[key] ?? 0;
                    final pct = total == 0 ? 0 : (n * 100 / total).round();
                    return Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          width: 8,
                          height: 8,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: _sentimentColor(key),
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Text('${_sentimentLabel(key, l10n)} $pct%', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                      ],
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(trend.icon, size: 16, color: trend.color),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Text(
                      trend.label,
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: trend.color, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    Text(
                      l10n.basedOnMessages(total),
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.grey),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\doctor\doctor_appointments_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../services/booking_service.dart';
import '../chat/chat_screen.dart';
import 'patient_records_screen.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class DoctorAppointmentsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const DoctorAppointmentsScreen({super.key});

  // Lower number = shown first. Anything without a known urgency
  // (e.g. patient hasn't submitted a questionnaire yet) sorts last.
  // These stay as the raw English values written by questionnaire_service —
  // only the label shown to the user is localized (see _urgencyLabel).
  static const Map<String, int> _urgencyPriority = {
    'Urgent': 0,
    'Moderate': 1,
    'Routine': 2,
  };

  Color? _urgencyColor(String? urgency) {
    switch (urgency) {
      case 'Urgent':
        return Colors.red;
      case 'Moderate':
        return Colors.orange;
      case 'Routine':
        return Colors.green;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  }

  String _urgencyLabel(String urgency, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (urgency) {
      case 'Urgent':
        return l10n.urgencyUrgent;
      case 'Moderate':
        return l10n.urgencyModerate;
      case 'Routine':
        return l10n.urgencyRoutine;
      default:
        return urgency;
    }
  }

  String _statusLabel(String status, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (status) {
      case 'confirmed':
        return l10n.statusConfirmedLabel;
      case 'cancelled':
        return l10n.statusCancelledLabel;
      default:
        return l10n.statusPendingLabel;
    }
  }

  Widget _buildUrgencyBadge(String urgency, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final color = _urgencyColor(urgency) ?? Colors.grey;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: color),
      ),
      child: Text(
        _urgencyLabel(urgency, l10n),
        style: TextStyle(color: color, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Looks up each patient's most recent questionnaire urgency label,
  /// so the appointment list can be sorted/color-coded by it.
  /// Patients with no questionnaire on file simply won't appear in the map.
  Future<Map<String, String>> _fetchUrgencyMap(List<String> patientIds) async {
    final uniqueIds = patientIds.toSet();
    final Map<String, String> result = {};

    await Future.wait(uniqueIds.map((id) async {
      final snap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('questionnaireResponses')
          .where('patientId', isEqualTo: id)
          .orderBy('analyzedAt', descending: true)
          .limit(1)
          .get();
      if (snap.docs.isNotEmpty) {
        final urgency = snap.docs.first.data()['urgency'] as String?;
        if (urgency != null) {
          result[id] = urgency;
        }
      }
    }));

    return result;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final BookingService bookingService = BookingService();
    final doctorId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.appointmentRequestsTitle)),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: bookingService.getDoctorBookings(doctorId),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return Center(child: Text(l10n.noAppointmentsYet));
          }

          final bookings = snapshot.data!.docs;
          final patientIds = bookings
              .map((d) => (d.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['patientId'] as String? ?? '')
              .where((id) => id.isNotEmpty)
              .toList();

          return FutureBuilder<Map<String, String>>(
            future: _fetchUrgencyMap(patientIds),
            builder: (context, urgencySnapshot) {
              // While urgency data loads, show the list unsorted rather
              // than blocking on a spinner — it will re-sort itself once
              // the map arrives.
              final urgencyMap = urgencySnapshot.data ?? const <String, String>{};

              final sortedBookings = [...bookings];
              sortedBookings.sort((a, b) {
                final aId = (a.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['patientId'] ?? '';
                final bId = (b.data() as Map<String, dynamic>)['patientId'] ?? '';
                final aPriority = _urgencyPriority[urgencyMap[aId]] ?? 99;
                final bPriority = _urgencyPriority[urgencyMap[bId]] ?? 99;
                return aPriority.compareTo(bPriority);
              });

              return ListView.builder(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                itemCount: sortedBookings.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final doc = sortedBookings[index];
                  final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                  final dateTime = (data['dateTime'] as Timestamp).toDate();
                  final status = data['status'] ?? 'pending';
                  final patientId = data['patientId'] ?? '';
                  final urgency = urgencyMap[patientId];
                  final urgencyColor = _urgencyColor(urgency);

                  return Card(
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                      side: BorderSide(
                        color: urgencyColor ?? Colors.transparent,
                        width: urgency == 'Urgent' ? 2 : 1,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              // Fetch patient name
                              Expanded(
                                child: FutureBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
                                  future: FirebaseFirestore.instance
                                      .collection('users')
                                      .doc(patientId)
                                      .get(),
                                  builder: (context, patientSnapshot) {
                                    String patientName = l10n.loadingLabel;
                                    if (patientSnapshot.hasData && patientSnapshot.data!.exists) {
                                      final pData = patientSnapshot.data!.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                                      patientName = pData['name'] ?? l10n.unknownPatient;
                                    }
                                    return Text(
                                      patientName,
                                      style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                                    );
                                  },
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (urgency != null) _buildUrgencyBadge(urgency, l10n),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text('${dateTime.day}/${dateTime.month}/${dateTime.year} at ${dateTime.hour}:${dateTime.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text(l10n.statusLabel(_statusLabel(status, l10n)),
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: status == 'confirmed'
                                    ? Colors.green
                                    : status == 'cancelled'
                                    ? Colors.red
                                    : Colors.orange,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              )),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          OutlinedButton.icon(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline),
                            label: Text(l10n.messagePatient),
                            onPressed: () {
                              Navigator.push(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => ChatScreen(
                                    otherUserId: patientId,
                                    otherUserName: l10n.rolePatient,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          OutlinedButton.icon(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.folder_shared),
                            label: Text(l10n.viewAddRecords),
                            onPressed: () {
                              Navigator.push(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => PatientRecordsScreen(
                                    patientId: patientId,
                                    patientName: l10n.rolePatient,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                          if (status == 'pending') ...[
                            const SizedBox(height: 8),
                            Row(
                              children: [
                                ElevatedButton(
                                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green),
                                  onPressed: () => bookingService.updateBookingStatus(doc.id, 'confirmed'),
                                  child: Text(l10n.acceptLabel),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                OutlinedButton(
                                  onPressed: () => bookingService.updateBookingStatus(doc.id, 'cancelled'),
                                  child: Text(l10n.rejectLabel),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class DoctorHomeScreen extends StatelessWidget {

      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,

class _ActionTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final Color iconColor;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _ActionTile({
    required this.icon,
    required this.iconColor,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade200),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              height: 48, width: 48,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: iconColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: Icon(icon, color: iconColor),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 14),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Text(subtitle, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, color: Colors.grey.shade400),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

# FILE: lib\screens\doctor\patient_records_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../services/medical_record_service.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'ai_summary_card.dart';
import 'chat_sentiment_summary_card.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class PatientRecordsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final String patientId;
  final String patientName;

  const PatientRecordsScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.patientId,
    required this.patientName,
  });

  void _showAddRecordDialog(BuildContext context, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final titleController = TextEditingController();
    final descriptionController = TextEditingController();
    final recordService = MedicalRecordService();
    final doctorId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: Text(l10n.addMedicalRecordTitle),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: titleController,
              decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.titleFieldLabel),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            TextField(
              controller: descriptionController,
              decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.descriptionFieldLabel),
              maxLines: 3,
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actionsPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: Text(l10n.cancel),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () async {
              if (titleController.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
              await recordService.addRecord(
                patientId: patientId,
                doctorId: doctorId,
                title: titleController.text.trim(),
                description: descriptionController.text.trim(),
              );
              if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
            },
            child: Text(l10n.save),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final MedicalRecordService recordService = MedicalRecordService();
    final doctorId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.recordsTitleFor(patientName))),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: () => _showAddRecordDialog(context, l10n),
        child: const Icon(Icons.add_rounded),
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: recordService.getPatientRecords(patientId),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }

          final records = snapshot.hasData ? snapshot.data!.docs : [];

          return ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 90),
            children: [
              // AI-generated pre-appointment summary (FR-D-02)
              AiSummaryCard(patientId: patientId),
              // Chat tone/sentiment trend summary — only shows content
              // once the patient has consented AND sent a few messages
              // to THIS doctor.
              ChatSentimentSummaryCard(patientId: patientId, doctorId: doctorId),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              Text(l10n.medicalRecordsTitle, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              if (records.isEmpty)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 30),
                  child: Center(
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        Icon(Icons.folder_off_outlined, size: 40, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                        const SizedBox(height: 10),
                        Text(l10n.noRecordsYet, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              else
                ...records.map((doc) {
                  final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                  final createdAt = data['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
                  final dateStr = createdAt != null
                      ? '${createdAt.toDate().day}/${createdAt.toDate().month}/${createdAt.toDate().year}'
                      : '';

                  return Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                      child: Row(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            height: 44, width: 44,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            ),
                            child: const Icon(Icons.description_rounded, color: AppTheme.primaryColor, size: 20),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 14),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Text(data['title'] ?? l10n.untitled,
                                    style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                Text(
                                  data['description'] ?? '',
                                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: Colors.grey.shade600, height: 1.4),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                Text(dateStr, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Colors.grey.shade400)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\patient\book_appointment_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../services/booking_service.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class BookAppointmentScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String doctorId;
  final String doctorName;

  const BookAppointmentScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.doctorId,
    required this.doctorName,
  });

  @override
  State<BookAppointmentScreen> createState() => _BookAppointmentScreenState();
}

class _BookAppointmentScreenState extends State<BookAppointmentScreen> {
  final BookingService _bookingService = BookingService();
  DateTime? _selectedDate;
  TimeOfDay? _selectedTime;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  Future<void> _pickDate() async {
    final picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 1)),
      firstDate: DateTime.now(),
      lastDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 60)),
    );
    if (picked != null) setState(() => _selectedDate = picked);
  }

  Future<void> _pickTime() async {
    final picked = await showTimePicker(
      context: context,
      initialTime: const TimeOfDay(hour: 10, minute: 0),
    );
    if (picked != null) setState(() => _selectedTime = picked);
  }

  Future<void> _confirmBooking() async {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    if (_selectedDate == null || _selectedTime == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(l10n.pleaseSelectDateTime)),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    final dateTime = DateTime(
      _selectedDate!.year,
      _selectedDate!.month,
      _selectedDate!.day,
      _selectedTime!.hour,
      _selectedTime!.minute,
    );

    final patientId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;
    final error = await _bookingService.bookAppointment(
      patientId: patientId,
      doctorId: widget.doctorId,
      dateTime: dateTime,
    );

    setState(() => _isLoading = false);

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (error == null) {
      Navigator.pop(context);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(l10n.appointmentRequestSent)),
      );
    } else {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text(l10n.errorPrefix(error))),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.bookAppointmentTitle)),
      body: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade200),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    height: 48, width: 48,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.medical_services_rounded, color: AppTheme.primaryColor),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 14),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(l10n.drPrefix(widget.doctorName), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                        Text(l10n.generalPractitioner, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 28),
            Text(l10n.selectDateTimeTitle, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            _PickerTile(
              icon: Icons.calendar_today_rounded,
              label: _selectedDate == null
                  ? l10n.selectDate
                  : '${_selectedDate!.day}/${_selectedDate!.month}/${_selectedDate!.year}',
              filled: _selectedDate != null,
              onTap: _pickDate,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _PickerTile(
              icon: Icons.access_time_rounded,
              label: _selectedTime == null ? l10n.selectTime : _selectedTime!.format(context),
              filled: _selectedTime != null,
              onTap: _pickTime,
            ),
            const Spacer(),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _confirmBooking,
              child: _isLoading
                  ? const SizedBox(
                height: 20, width: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white),
              )
                  : Text(l10n.confirmAppointment),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PickerTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final bool filled;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _PickerTile({required this.icon, required this.label, required this.filled, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: filled ? AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.06) : Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          border: Border.all(color: filled ? AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3) : Colors.grey.shade200),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: filled ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade500, size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text(label,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  color: filled ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade600,
                )),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
