# FILE: lib\screens\patient\medical_records_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../services/medical_record_service.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class MedicalRecordsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const MedicalRecordsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final MedicalRecordService recordService = MedicalRecordService();
    final patientId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.myMedicalRecordsTitle)),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: recordService.getPatientRecords(patientId),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.folder_off_outlined, size: 44, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Text(l10n.noMedicalRecordsYet, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          final records = snapshot.data!.docs;

          return ListView.builder(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            itemCount: records.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final data = records[index].data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              final createdAt = data['createdAt'] as Timestamp?;
              final dateStr = createdAt != null
                  ? '${createdAt.toDate().day}/${createdAt.toDate().month}/${createdAt.toDate().year}'
                  : '';

              return Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        height: 44, width: 44,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: const Color(0xFF6366F1).withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        ),
                        child: const Icon(Icons.description_rounded, color: Color(0xFF6366F1), size: 20),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 14),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(data['title'] ?? l10n.untitled,
                                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            Text(
                              data['description'] ?? '',
                              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: Colors.grey.shade600, height: 1.4),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 8),
                            Text(dateStr, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Colors.grey.shade400)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\patient\my_appointments_screen.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import '../../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../../services/booking_service.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class MyAppointmentsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const MyAppointmentsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final BookingService bookingService = BookingService();
    final patientId = AuthService().currentUser!.uid;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.myAppointmentsTitle)),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: bookingService.getPatientBookings(patientId),
        builder: (context, snapshot) {
          if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.event_busy_rounded, size: 44, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Text(l10n.noAppointmentsYet, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500)),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          final bookings = snapshot.data!.docs;

          return ListView.builder(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            itemCount: bookings.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final doc = bookings[index];
              final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
              final dateTime = (data['dateTime'] as Timestamp).toDate();
              final status = data['status'] ?? 'pending';

              final statusInfo = _statusInfo(status, l10n);

              return Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        height: 48, width: 48,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: statusInfo.color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        ),
                        child: Icon(Icons.event_rounded, color: statusInfo.color),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 14),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              '${dateTime.day}/${dateTime.month}/${dateTime.year} · ${dateTime.hour}:${dateTime.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}',
                              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 14),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 6),
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: statusInfo.color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                statusInfo.label,
                                style: TextStyle(color: statusInfo.color, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (status == 'pending' || status == 'confirmed')
                        IconButton(
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, color: Colors.redAccent),
                          onPressed: () => bookingService.cancelBooking(doc.id),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  ({Color color, String label}) _statusInfo(String status, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (status) {
      case 'confirmed':
        return (color: const Color(0xFF16A34A), label: l10n.statusConfirmedLabel);
      case 'cancelled':
        return (color: const Color(0xFFDC2626), label: l10n.statusCancelledLabel);
      default:
        return (color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B), label: l10n.statusPendingLabel);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

import 'book_appointment_screen.dart';

import 'medical_records_screen.dart';
import 'questionnaire_screen.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class PatientHomeScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const PatientHomeScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final BookingService bookingService = BookingService();
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      body: CustomScrollView(
        slivers: [
          SliverAppBar(
            pinned: true,
            backgroundColor: Colors.white,
            elevation: 0,
            expandedHeight: 150,
            actions: [
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.language_rounded, color: Colors.grey),
                tooltip: l10n.languageLabel,
                onPressed: () {
                  Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SettingsScreen()));
                },
              ),
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.logout, color: Colors.grey),
                onPressed: () async {
                  await AuthService().signOut();
                  if (context.mounted) {
                    Navigator.pushReplacement(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const LoginScreen()));
                  }
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
            ],
            flexibleSpace: FlexibleSpaceBar(
              titlePadding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 24, bottom: 16),
              title: Text(l10n.findADoctorTitle, style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF1E293B), fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
              background: Container(color: Colors.white),
            ),
          ),
          SliverToBoxAdapter(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 4),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: _QuickAction(
                      icon: Icons.assignment_outlined,
                      label: l10n.questionnaireLabel,
                      color: const Color(0xFF6366F1),
                      onTap: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const QuestionnaireScreen())),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                    child: _QuickAction(
                      icon: Icons.folder_shared_outlined,
                      label: l10n.recordsLabel,
                      color: const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
                      onTap: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const MedicalRecordsScreen())),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                    child: _QuickAction(
                      icon: Icons.calendar_month_outlined,
                      label: l10n.appointmentsLabel,
                      color: AppTheme.primaryColor,
                      onTap: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const MyAppointmentsScreen())),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          SliverToBoxAdapter(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 20, 20, 8),
              child: Text(l10n.availableDoctorsTitle, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge),
            ),
          ),
          StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: bookingService.getDoctors(),
            builder: (context, snapshot) {
              if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                return const SliverFillRemaining(child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
              }
              if (!snapshot.hasData || snapshot.data!.docs.isEmpty) {
                return SliverFillRemaining(
                  child: Center(child: Text(l10n.noDoctorsYet)),
                );
              }

              final doctors = snapshot.data!.docs;

              return SliverPadding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 20),
                sliver: SliverList(
                  delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                        (context, index) {
                      final doc = doctors[index];
                      final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
                      final name = data['name'] ?? l10n.unknownLabel;

                      return Card(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Container(
                                height: 52, width: 52,
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                                ),
                                child: const Icon(Icons.medical_services_rounded, color: AppTheme.primaryColor),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 14),
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(l10n.drPrefix(name), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                    Text(l10n.generalPractitioner, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 12.5)),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                              IconButton(
                                icon: const Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline_rounded, color: AppTheme.primaryColor),
                                onPressed: () {
                                  Navigator.push(
                                    context,
                                    MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ChatScreen(otherUserId: doc.id, otherUserName: l10n.drPrefix(name))),
                                  );
                                },
                              ),
                              ElevatedButton(
                                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 12)),
                                onPressed: () {
                                  Navigator.push(
                                    context,
                                    MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => BookAppointmentScreen(doctorId: doc.id, doctorName: name)),
                                  );
                                },
                                child: Text(l10n.bookLabel),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                    childCount: doctors.length,
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _QuickAction extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _QuickAction({required this.icon, required this.label, required this.color, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text(label, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, color: color)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\screens\patient\questionnaire_screen.dart
################################################################################

// lib/screens/patient/questionnaire_screen.dart
//
// FR-P-05: Patient fills a pre-appointment questionnaire; answers are
// analyzed by AI into a summary for the doctor, plus a cautious triage
// suggestion shown to the patient (inspired by thesis §2.5 / HealthTap
// Dr. A.I. — this is NOT a diagnosis, just a routing suggestion).

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../services/questionnaire_service.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

class QuestionnaireScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const QuestionnaireScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<QuestionnaireScreen> createState() => _QuestionnaireScreenState();
}

class _QuestionnaireScreenState extends State<QuestionnaireScreen> {
  final _service = QuestionnaireService();
  final _complaintController = TextEditingController();

  bool _loadingQuestions = false;
  bool _submitting = false;

  List<Question> _questions = [];
  final Map<String, String> _answers = {};
  QuestionnaireResult? _result;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _complaintController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _generateQuestions() async {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final complaint = _complaintController.text.trim();
    if (complaint.isEmpty) {
      _showError(l10n.pleaseDescribeSymptom);
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _loadingQuestions = true);
    try {
      final questions = await _service.generateQuestions(complaint);
      setState(() {
        _questions = questions;
        _loadingQuestions = false;
      });
    } on QuestionnaireException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _loadingQuestions = false);
      _showError(e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _loadingQuestions = false);
      _showError(l10n.somethingWentWrong);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    if (_answers.length < _questions.length ||
        _answers.values.any((v) => v.trim().isEmpty)) {
      _showError(l10n.pleaseAnswerAll);
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _submitting = true);
    try {
      final answersList = _questions
          .map((q) => {'question': q.text, 'answer': _answers[q.id] ?? ''})
          .toList();
      final result = await _service.submitAnswers(
        chiefComplaint: _complaintController.text.trim(),
        answers: answersList,
      );
      setState(() {
        _submitting = false;
        _result = result;
      });
    } on QuestionnaireException catch (e) {
      setState(() => _submitting = false);
      _showError(e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() => _submitting = false);
      _showError(l10n.couldNotSubmit);
    }
  }

  void _showError(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
  }

  Widget _buildQuestionInput(Question q, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    switch (q.type) {
      case 'yes_no':
        return Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: RadioListTile<String>(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                title: Text(l10n.yesLabel),
                value: 'Yes',
                groupValue: _answers[q.id],
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _answers[q.id] = v!),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: RadioListTile<String>(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                title: Text(l10n.noLabel),
                value: 'No',
                groupValue: _answers[q.id],
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _answers[q.id] = v!),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        );
      case 'scale_1_10':
        final current = int.tryParse(_answers[q.id] ?? '') ?? 5;
        return Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Slider(
                min: 1,
                max: 10,
                divisions: 9,
                value: current.toDouble(),
                label: current.toString(),
                onChanged: (v) =>
                    setState(() => _answers[q.id] = v.round().toString()),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 24, child: Text('$current')),
          ],
        );
      default:
        return TextField(
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            hintText: l10n.yourAnswerHint,
            border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
          ),
          onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _answers[q.id] = v),
        );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.preAppointmentQuestionnaireTitle)),
      body: _result != null ? _buildResult(_result!, l10n) : _buildForm(l10n),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildForm(AppLocalizations l10n) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(
            l10n.describeSymptomPrompt,
            style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          TextField(
            controller: _complaintController,
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              hintText: l10n.symptomHint,
              border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
            ),
            maxLines: 2,
            enabled: _questions.isEmpty,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          if (_questions.isEmpty)
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _loadingQuestions ? null : _generateQuestions,
              child: _loadingQuestions
                  ? const SizedBox(
                height: 18,
                width: 18,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
              )
                  : Text(l10n.continueLabel),
            ),
          if (_questions.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const Divider(height: 32),
            ..._questions.map(
                  (q) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 20),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(q.text,
                        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    _buildQuestionInput(q, l10n),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: _submitting ? null : _submit,
              child: _submitting
                  ? const SizedBox(
                height: 18,
                width: 18,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
              )
                  : Text(l10n.submitQuestionnaire),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildResult(QuestionnaireResult result, AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final (color, icon, label) = switch (result.triageLevel) {
      TriageLevel.emergency => (Colors.red, Icons.emergency, l10n.seekEmergencyCare),
      TriageLevel.seeDoctor => (Colors.orange, Icons.medical_information, l10n.seeADoctor),
      TriageLevel.selfCare => (Colors.green, Icons.self_improvement, l10n.selfCareSuggested),
    };

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: color, width: 1.5),
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Icon(icon, color: color, size: 48),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(
                  label,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: color,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  result.triageAdvice,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15),
                ),
                if (result.triageLevel == TriageLevel.emergency) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(
                    l10n.emergencyNote,
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text(
            l10n.automatedSuggestionNote,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.grey),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: Text(l10n.doneLabel),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// lib/screens/settings/settings_screen.dart
//
// User-facing language switcher (English / Urdu). Reachable right now
// from the language icon on WelcomeScreen; once the rest of the app's
// screens are localized (Phase 2), add the same icon/entry to the
// patient/doctor/admin home screens' app bars too.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import '../../locale_provider.dart';
import '../../theme/app_theme.dart';

class SettingsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const SettingsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.backgroundColor,
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.settingsTitle)),
      body: ListenableBuilder(
        listenable: localeProvider,
        builder: (context, _) {
          final currentCode = localeProvider.locale.languageCode;

          return ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            children: [
              Text(
                l10n.languageLabel,
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                l10n.languageSectionHint,
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: Colors.grey.shade600),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              _LanguageOption(
                label: l10n.languageEnglish,
                selected: currentCode == 'en',
                onTap: () => localeProvider.setLocale(const Locale('en')),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              _LanguageOption(
                label: l10n.languageUrdu,
                selected: currentCode == 'ur',
                onTap: () => localeProvider.setLocale(const Locale('ur')),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _LanguageOption extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _LanguageOption({required this.label, required this.selected, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          border: Border.all(color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade200, width: 1.4),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(
              selected ? Icons.radio_button_checked : Icons.radio_button_off,
              color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade400,
              size: 20,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryColor : Colors.grey.shade800,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class AdminService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // Get all users (patients + doctors)
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getAllUsers() {
    return _firestore.collection('users').orderBy('createdAt', descending: true).snapshots();
  }

  // Update a user's info
  Future<void> updateUser(String userId, Map<String, dynamic> data) async {
    await _firestore.collection('users').doc(userId).update(data);
  }

  // Delete a user's Firestore record
  // Note: this removes their profile data. Deleting the actual login
  // account requires Firebase Admin SDK (a backend), which is outside
  // this app's scope, but is fine to mention as a known limitation.
  Future<void> deleteUser(String userId) async {
    await _firestore.collection('users').doc(userId).delete();
  }

  // Get all payments
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getAllPayments() {
    return _firestore.collection('payments').orderBy('processedAt', descending: true).snapshots();
  }

  // Add a payment record
  Future<void> addPayment({
    required String bookingId,
    required double amount,
    required String status,
  }) async {
    await _firestore.collection('payments').add({
      'bookingId': bookingId,
      'amount': amount,
      'currency': 'PKR',
      'status': status,
      'processedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  // Update payment status (confirm / refund)
  Future<void> updatePaymentStatus(String paymentId, String status) async {
    await _firestore.collection('payments').doc(paymentId).update({'status': status});
  }

  // Simple log entry writer, used across admin actions
  Future<void> writeLog({
    required String entity,
    required String entityId,
    required String action,
    required String performedBy,
  }) async {
    await _firestore.collection('logs').add({
      'entity': entity,
      'entityId': entityId,
      'action': action,
      'performedBy': performedBy,
      'timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  // Get logs
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getLogs() {
    return _firestore.collection('logs').orderBy('timestamp', descending: true).snapshots();
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\services\auth_service.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class AuthService {
  final FirebaseAuth _auth = FirebaseAuth.instance;
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // Current logged-in user
  User? get currentUser => _auth.currentUser;

  // Sign up a new user (patient or doctor)
  Future<String?> signUp({
    required String name,
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String role, // "patient" or "doctor"
  }) async {
    try {
      UserCredential result = await _auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
        email: email,
        password: password,
      );

      // Save extra user info in Firestore
      await _firestore.collection('users').doc(result.user!.uid).set({
        'userId': result.user!.uid,
        'name': name,
        'email': email,
        'role': role,
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });

      return null; // null means success
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      return e.message ?? 'Signup failed';
    }
  }

  // Log in existing user
  Future<String?> logIn({
    required String email,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    try {
      await _auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email: email, password: password);
      return null; // success
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      return e.message ?? 'Login failed';
    }
  }

  // Get the role of the currently logged-in user
  Future<String?> getUserRole(String uid) async {
    DocumentSnapshot doc = await _firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    if (doc.exists) {
      return doc.get('role') as String;
    }
    return null;
  }

  // Log out
  Future<void> signOut() async {
    await _auth.signOut();
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\services\booking_service.dart
################################################################################

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class BookingService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // Get all doctors
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getDoctors() {
    return _firestore
        .collection('users')
        .where('role', isEqualTo: 'doctor')
        .snapshots();
  }

  // Book an appointment.
  // Also writes a `doctorPatientLinks/{doctorId}_{patientId}` doc in the
  // same atomic batch. That link doc is what firestore.rules checks
  // (via exists()) to prove "this doctor is assigned to this patient" —
  // it's how the doctor is later allowed to read this patient's
  // medicalRecords and questionnaireResponses. Doing it client-side in a
  // batch (instead of a Cloud Function trigger) avoids needing the
  // Blaze plan.
  Future<String?> bookAppointment({
    required String patientId,
    required String doctorId,
    required DateTime dateTime,
  }) async {
    try {
      final bookingRef = _firestore.collection('bookings').doc();
      final linkRef = _firestore
          .collection('doctorPatientLinks')
          .doc('${doctorId}_$patientId');

      final batch = _firestore.batch();

      batch.set(bookingRef, {
        'patientId': patientId,
        'doctorId': doctorId,
        'dateTime': Timestamp.fromDate(dateTime),
        'status': 'pending',
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });

      batch.set(linkRef, {
        'doctorId': doctorId,
        'patientId': patientId,
        'lastBookingId': bookingRef.id,
        'updatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      }, SetOptions(merge: true));

      await batch.commit();
      return null; // success
    } catch (e) {
      return e.toString();
    }
  }

  // Get bookings for a patient
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getPatientBookings(String patientId) {
    return _firestore
        .collection('bookings')
        .where('patientId', isEqualTo: patientId)
        .orderBy('dateTime')
        .snapshots();
  }

  // Get bookings for a doctor
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getDoctorBookings(String doctorId) {
    return _firestore
        .collection('bookings')
        .where('doctorId', isEqualTo: doctorId)
        .orderBy('dateTime')
        .snapshots();
  }

  // Cancel a booking
  Future<void> cancelBooking(String bookingId) async {
    await _firestore.collection('bookings').doc(bookingId).update({
      'status': 'cancelled',
    });
  }

  // Doctor accepts/rejects a booking
  Future<void> updateBookingStatus(String bookingId, String status) async {
    await _firestore.collection('bookings').doc(bookingId).update({
      'status': status,
    });
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\services\chat_service.dart
################################################################################

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

class ChatService {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  // ---- AI tone/sentiment analysis (Groq) ---------------------------------
  //
  // Reads the CONTENT of a user's chat messages to give the doctor a quick
  // "how are they doing" indicator (Distressed / Neutral / Calm). This
  // reads personal message content, so it is ONLY run for a sender who has
  // explicitly given consent — see getSentimentConsent/setSentimentConsent.
  static const String _groqApiKey = String.fromEnvironment(
    'GROQ_API_KEY',
    defaultValue: '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',
  );
  static const String _groqUrl = 'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions';
  static const String _groqModel = 'openai/gpt-oss-120b';
  static const List<String> _validSentiments = ['Distressed', 'Neutral', 'Calm'];

  // Creates a consistent, unique chat ID for any 2 users (order doesn't matter)
  String getChatId(String userId1, String userId2) {
    List<String> ids = [userId1, userId2];
    ids.sort();
    return ids.join('_');
  }

  // ---- Privacy consent for sentiment analysis ----------------------------

  /// Returns null if this user has never been asked yet, true if they
  /// agreed to AI tone analysis of their own messages, false if they
  /// declined. Must be checked BEFORE analyzing any of their messages.
  Future<bool?> getSentimentConsent(String userId) async {
    final doc = await _firestore.collection('users').doc(userId).get();
    final value = doc.data()?['chatSentimentConsent'];
    return value is bool ? value : null;
  }

  /// Records the user's consent choice.
  Future<void> setSentimentConsent(String userId, bool consent) async {
    await _firestore.collection('users').doc(userId).set({
      'chatSentimentConsent': consent,
      'chatSentimentConsentAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
  }

  Future<String?> _analyzeSentiment(String content) async {
    final body = {
      'model': _groqModel,
      'messages': [
        {
          'role': 'user',
          'content':
          'Classify the overall emotional tone of this chat message from a '
              'patient to their doctor. Respond with ONLY valid JSON, no '
              'markdown, matching exactly {"sentiment":"Distressed"|"Neutral"|"Calm"}.\n\n'
              'Message: "$content"'
        }
      ],
      'temperature': 0.2,
      'response_format': {'type': 'json_object'},
    };

    try {
      final resp = await http.post(
        Uri.parse(_groqUrl),
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Authorization': 'Bearer $_groqApiKey',
        },
        body: jsonEncode(body),
      );
      if (resp.statusCode != 200) {
        // ignore: avoid_print
        print('Sentiment Groq error: ${resp.statusCode} ${resp.body}');
        return null;
      }
      final data = jsonDecode(resp.body);
      final text = data?['choices']?[0]?['message']?['content'] as String?;
      if (text == null) return null;
      final parsed = jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final sentiment = (parsed['sentiment'] as String? ?? '').trim();
      return _validSentiments.contains(sentiment) ? sentiment : null;
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore: avoid_print
      print('Sentiment analysis failed: $e');
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Fire-and-forget: runs AFTER the message is already sent, then patches
  /// the same message document with the result. Never blocks sending and
  /// never throws — a failed/skipped analysis just leaves no sentiment tag,
  /// so chat always keeps working even if this fails.
  void _analyzeAndAttachSentiment(DocumentReference docRef, String content) {
    _analyzeSentiment(content).then((sentiment) {
      if (sentiment != null) {
        docRef.update({'sentiment': sentiment});
      }
    });
  }

  // Send a message
  Future<void> sendMessage({
    required String senderId,
    required String receiverId,
    required String content,
  }) async {
    final chatId = getChatId(senderId, receiverId);

    final docRef = await _firestore
        .collection('chats')
        .doc(chatId)
        .collection('messages')
        .add({
      'senderId': senderId,
      'receiverId': receiverId,
      'content': content,
      'sentAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'sentiment': null,
    });

    // Only analyze this message if the SENDER has explicitly opted in.
    final consent = await getSentimentConsent(senderId);
    if (consent == true) {
      _analyzeAndAttachSentiment(docRef, content);
    }

    // Also save a summary doc so we can list conversations later
    await _firestore.collection('chats').doc(chatId).set({
      'participants': [senderId, receiverId],
      'lastMessage': content,
      'lastMessageAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
  }

  /// Streams the messages between two users, oldest first, so a
  /// StreamBuilder/ListView can render them straight down the screen.
  Stream<QuerySnapshot> getMessages(String userId1, String userId2) {
    final chatId = getChatId(userId1, userId2);
    return _firestore
        .collection('chats')
        .doc(chatId)
        .collection('messages')
        .orderBy('sentAt', descending: false)
        .snapshots();
  }

  /// Deletes a single message. Only call this for a message the CURRENT
  /// user themselves sent (check `isMe` in the UI before showing the
  /// option) — also enforced by firestore.rules (sender-only delete).
  Future<void> deleteMessage({
    required String userId1,
    required String userId2,
    required String messageId,
  }) async {
    final chatId = getChatId(userId1, userId2);
    await _firestore
        .collection('chats')
        .doc(chatId)
        .collection('messages')
        .doc(messageId)
        .delete();
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\services\questionnaire_service.dart
################################################################################

// lib/services/questionnaire_service.dart
//
// Calls Groq's API directly from the Flutter app (no Firebase Cloud
// Functions / Blaze plan required). Meant for FYP/demo use.
//
// ⚠️ SECURITY NOTE: The Groq API key below is embedded in the client and
// can be extracted by anyone who decompiles the app. This is acceptable
// for a student project demo, but NOT recommended for production apps.
// To reduce risk:
//   - Set a low rate limit / spend cap on this key in the Groq dashboard
//     (console.groq.com/settings/limits)
//   - Rotate the key before public release or after submission
//   - Never commit the key to a public GitHub repo (use --dart-define,
//     see notes at the bottom of this file)

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';

class Question {
  final String id;
  final String text;
  final String type; // "text" | "yes_no" | "scale_1_10"

  Question({required this.id, required this.text, required this.type});

  factory Question.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return Question(
      id: map['id']?.toString() ?? '',
      text: map['text']?.toString() ?? '',
      type: map['type']?.toString() ?? 'text',
    );
  }
}

/// Patient-facing triage routing suggestion. NOT a diagnosis — just a
/// cautious "who should look at this, and how soon" suggestion, shown to
/// the patient right after they submit the questionnaire.
enum TriageLevel { emergency, seeDoctor, selfCare }

/// Result returned after a questionnaire is analyzed by the AI.
/// Bundles the doctor-facing summary/urgency/progress note AND the
/// patient-facing triage suggestion, so both screens can use one call.
class QuestionnaireResult {
  /// Doctor-facing clinical-style summary (shown on ai_summary_card.dart).
  /// Always in English, regardless of what language the patient wrote in.
  final String summary;

  /// Doctor-facing urgency label used to sort/color the doctor's
  /// appointment list: "Urgent" | "Moderate" | "Routine".
  final String urgency;

  /// Doctor-facing trend note comparing this submission to past ones.
  /// Null when this is the patient's first submission.
  final String? progressNote;

  /// Patient-facing triage bucket, derived from [urgency].
  final TriageLevel triageLevel;

  /// Patient-facing plain-language advice, written in the SAME language
  /// the patient used (English, Urdu, or Roman Urdu).
  final String triageAdvice;

  /// English translation of the patient's chief complaint, for the
  /// doctor's benefit when the patient wrote in Urdu/Roman Urdu. Equal to
  /// the original complaint when it was already in English.
  final String translatedComplaint;

  QuestionnaireResult({
    required this.summary,
    required this.urgency,
    this.progressNote,
    required this.triageLevel,
    required this.triageAdvice,
    required this.translatedComplaint,
  });
}

class QuestionnaireException implements Exception {
  final String message;
  QuestionnaireException(this.message);
  @override
  String toString() => message;
}

class QuestionnaireService {
  // TODO: paste your Groq key here, or better, pass it via
  // --dart-define=GROQ_API_KEY=xxxx (see bottom of file).
  static const String _groqApiKey = String.fromEnvironment(
    'GROQ_API_KEY',
    defaultValue: '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',
  );

  static const String _groqUrl =
      'https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions';
  static const String _groqModel = 'openai/gpt-oss-120b';

  /// Valid urgency labels. Anything else returned by the AI is coerced
  /// to "Routine" so a bad/unexpected AI response never crashes the UI
  /// or silently hides a patient from the doctor's list.
  static const List<String> _validUrgencyLabels = ['Urgent', 'Moderate', 'Routine'];

  /// How many of the patient's most recent past responses to feed back
  /// to the AI as context when building a progress/trend note.
  static const int _historyLookback = 3;

  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;
  final FirebaseAuth _auth;

  QuestionnaireService({
    FirebaseFirestore? firestore,
    FirebaseAuth? auth,
  })  : _firestore = firestore ?? FirebaseFirestore.instance,
        _auth = auth ?? FirebaseAuth.instance;

  Future<String> _callGroq(String prompt, {bool json = false}) async {
    if (_groqApiKey.isEmpty || _groqApiKey == 'PASTE_YOUR_GROQ_API_KEY_HERE') {
      throw QuestionnaireException(
        'Groq API key is not set. Add it in questionnaire_service.dart '
            'or pass --dart-define=GROQ_API_KEY=your_key when running.',
      );
    }

    final body = {
      'model': _groqModel,
      'messages': [
        {'role': 'user', 'content': prompt}
      ],
      'temperature': 0.4,
      if (json) 'response_format': {'type': 'json_object'},
    };

    http.Response resp;
    try {
      resp = await http.post(
        Uri.parse(_groqUrl),
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Authorization': 'Bearer $_groqApiKey',
        },
        body: jsonEncode(body),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('Could not reach the AI service: $e');
    }

    if (resp.statusCode != 200) {
      // ignore: avoid_print
      print('Groq API error: ${resp.statusCode} ${resp.body}');
      throw QuestionnaireException('The AI service failed to respond.');
    }

    final data = jsonDecode(resp.body);
    final text = data?['choices']?[0]?['message']?['content'] as String?;
    if (text == null || text.isEmpty) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('The AI service returned an empty response.');
    }
    return text;
  }

  /// Step 1: send the patient's chief complaint, get back AI-generated
  /// follow-up questions. If the complaint was written in Urdu or Roman
  /// Urdu, the questions come back in the same style so the patient can
  /// understand them — the doctor-facing content (Step 2) is always
  /// translated to English regardless.
  Future<List<Question>> generateQuestions(String chiefComplaint) async {
    final complaint = chiefComplaint.trim();
    if (complaint.isEmpty) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('chiefComplaint is required.');
    }
    if (complaint.length > 500) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('chiefComplaint is too long.');
    }

    final prompt =
    '''You are a medical intake assistant helping prepare a patient for a doctor's appointment.
Patient's chief complaint: "$complaint"

The patient may have written this in English, Urdu, or Roman Urdu (Urdu
written using English letters). Detect which one it is.

Generate 6 to 8 short, relevant pre-appointment screening questions a doctor
would want answered before seeing this patient. Do NOT attempt to diagnose or
suggest treatment — only ask clarifying intake questions (duration, severity,
triggers, associated symptoms, relevant history, etc).

Write the questions in the SAME language/style the patient used for their
complaint (English, Urdu script, or Roman Urdu) so the patient can easily
understand and answer them.

Return ONLY valid JSON (no markdown fences, no extra text) matching exactly:
{"questions":[{"id":"q1","text":"...","type":"text"}]}

"type" must be one of: "text", "yes_no", "scale_1_10".''';

    final raw = await _callGroq(prompt, json: true);

    Map<String, dynamic> parsed;
    try {
      parsed = jsonDecode(raw) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore: avoid_print
      print('Failed to parse Groq JSON: $raw');
      throw QuestionnaireException('The AI returned an unexpected format.');
    }

    final rawQuestions = parsed['questions'];
    if (rawQuestions is! List || rawQuestions.isEmpty) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('The AI did not return any questions.');
    }

    return rawQuestions
        .map((q) => Question.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(q as Map)))
        .toList();
  }

  /// Fetches the patient's most recent past questionnaire responses
  /// (excluding the one currently being submitted, since that hasn't
  /// been written yet) to use as context for progress/trend tracking.
  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> _fetchRecentHistory(String patientId) async {
    final snap = await _firestore
        .collection('questionnaireResponses')
        .where('patientId', isEqualTo: patientId)
        .orderBy('analyzedAt', descending: true)
        .limit(_historyLookback)
        .get();

    return snap.docs.map((d) => d.data()).toList();
  }

  String _formatHistoryForPrompt(List<Map<String, dynamic>> history) {
    if (history.isEmpty) return '';

    // history[0] is the most recent past submission.
    final buffer = StringBuffer();
    for (var i = 0; i < history.length; i++) {
      final entry = history[i];
      final ts = entry['analyzedAt'];
      String dateLabel = 'Previous visit ${i + 1}';
      if (ts is Timestamp) {
        final d = ts.toDate();
        dateLabel = '${d.year}-${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
      }
      // Use the English translation when available so history context
      // stays in English internally, even if older entries pre-date
      // this field (falls back to the raw complaint).
      final pastComplaint = entry['translatedComplaint'] ?? entry['chiefComplaint'] ?? '';
      buffer.writeln('[$dateLabel] Complaint: $pastComplaint');
      buffer.writeln('Summary: ${entry['analysisSummary'] ?? ''}');
      buffer.writeln();
    }
    return buffer.toString();
  }

  /// Maps the doctor-facing urgency label to a patient-facing triage
  /// bucket. Kept as a single mapping (rather than asking the AI for a
  /// separate, possibly-inconsistent classification) so the doctor's
  /// list and the patient's suggestion can never disagree with each other.
  TriageLevel _triageLevelFromUrgency(String urgency) {
    switch (urgency) {
      case 'Urgent':
        return TriageLevel.emergency;
      case 'Moderate':
        return TriageLevel.seeDoctor;
      case 'Routine':
      default:
        return TriageLevel.selfCare;
    }
  }

  /// Step 2: send the patient's answers, get back the AI clinical summary,
  /// urgency label, progress note, an English translation of the
  /// complaint, and a patient-facing triage message — then save the full
  /// record to Firestore.
  Future<QuestionnaireResult> submitAnswers({
    required String chiefComplaint,
    required List<Map<String, String>> answers,
  }) async {
    final user = _auth.currentUser;
    if (user == null) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('You must be logged in.');
    }

    final complaint = chiefComplaint.trim();
    if (complaint.isEmpty || answers.isEmpty) {
      throw QuestionnaireException(
        'chiefComplaint and a non-empty answers array are required.',
      );
    }

    final qaText = answers
        .asMap()
        .entries
        .map((e) =>
    '${e.key + 1}. ${e.value['question']}\n   Answer: ${e.value['answer']}')
        .join('\n');

    // Pull recent history BEFORE writing the new response, so it isn't
    // compared against itself.
    final history = await _fetchRecentHistory(user.uid);
    final historyText = _formatHistoryForPrompt(history);

    final prompt =
    '''You are assisting a doctor by summarizing a patient's pre-appointment
questionnaire and triaging its urgency. The patient's complaint and/or
answers may be in English, Urdu, or Roman Urdu — detect the language(s) used.

Chief complaint: "$complaint"

Patient answers:
$qaText

${historyText.isNotEmpty ? 'Patient\'s previous questionnaire submissions (most recent first), for context on how their condition is trending:\n$historyText\n' : ''}
Do FIVE things:
1. Write a concise clinical-style summary IN ENGLISH (max 150 words),
   written for the doctor, covering key symptoms, duration/severity if
   mentioned, and any notable red flags. Translate anything not already in
   English. Do NOT diagnose or recommend treatment — only summarize what
   the patient reported.
2. Assign an urgency label based only on what the patient reported:
   - "Urgent": possible red-flag symptoms, severe or rapidly worsening
     condition, anything that suggests the patient should be seen soon.
   - "Moderate": a real but non-emergency concern.
   - "Routine": mild, stable, or general check-up type concern.
${historyText.isNotEmpty ? '3. Write a short (max 40 words) progress note comparing this submission to the previous ones above — e.g. improving, worsening, or stable, and on what basis.' : '3. Set progressNote to null (this is the patient\'s first submission, so there is nothing to compare against).'}
4. Write a short (max 40 words) message spoken DIRECTLY to the patient
   (use "you"), IN THE SAME LANGUAGE/STYLE the patient used for their
   chief complaint (English, Urdu, or Roman Urdu) so they can easily
   understand it. Match the tone to the urgency label above. Be cautious
   rather than alarming: only word it like a possible emergency if the
   urgency is "Urgent" AND the reported symptoms genuinely sound severe.
   Never diagnose or name a specific condition — only suggest what to do
   next (e.g. wait for the appointment, contact the doctor sooner, or
   seek urgent in-person care).
5. Provide "translatedComplaint": an English translation of the chief
   complaint, so the doctor can read it even if the patient wrote in Urdu
   or Roman Urdu. If it was already in English, repeat it unchanged.

Return ONLY valid JSON (no markdown fences, no extra text) matching exactly:
{"summary":"...","urgency":"Urgent"|"Moderate"|"Routine","progressNote":"..." or null,"patientMessage":"...","translatedComplaint":"..."}''';

    final raw = await _callGroq(prompt, json: true);

    Map<String, dynamic> parsed;
    try {
      parsed = jsonDecode(raw) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore: avoid_print
      print('Failed to parse Groq JSON: $raw');
      throw QuestionnaireException('The AI returned an unexpected format.');
    }

    final summary = (parsed['summary'] as String? ?? '').trim();
    if (summary.isEmpty) {
      throw QuestionnaireException('The AI did not return a summary.');
    }

    var urgency = (parsed['urgency'] as String? ?? 'Routine').trim();
    if (!_validUrgencyLabels.contains(urgency)) {
      // ignore: avoid_print
      print('AI returned unexpected urgency label "$urgency", defaulting to Routine.');
      urgency = 'Routine';
    }

    final progressNoteRaw = parsed['progressNote'];
    final progressNote = (progressNoteRaw is String && progressNoteRaw.trim().isNotEmpty)
        ? progressNoteRaw.trim()
        : null;

    var patientMessage = (parsed['patientMessage'] as String? ?? '').trim();
    final triageLevel = _triageLevelFromUrgency(urgency);
    if (patientMessage.isEmpty) {
      // Fallback text if the AI omits it, so the UI never shows a blank card.
      patientMessage = switch (triageLevel) {
        TriageLevel.emergency =>
        'Your symptoms may need prompt attention. Please contact your doctor as soon as possible.',
        TriageLevel.seeDoctor =>
        'It would be a good idea to discuss this with your doctor soon.',
        TriageLevel.selfCare =>
        'Your symptoms sound manageable for now. Your doctor will review your full answers before your appointment.',
      };
    }

    // Fall back to the raw complaint if the AI omits the translation,
    // rather than showing the doctor a blank field.
    final translatedComplaint =
    (parsed['translatedComplaint'] as String? ?? '').trim().isNotEmpty
        ? (parsed['translatedComplaint'] as String).trim()
        : complaint;

    await _firestore.collection('questionnaireResponses').add({
      'patientId': user.uid,
      'chiefComplaint': complaint,
      'translatedComplaint': translatedComplaint,
      'answers': answers,
      'analysisSummary': summary,
      'urgency': urgency,
      'progressNote': progressNote,
      'patientMessage': patientMessage,
      'analyzedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });

    return QuestionnaireResult(
      summary: summary,
      urgency: urgency,
      progressNote: progressNote,
      triageLevel: triageLevel,
      triageAdvice: patientMessage,
      translatedComplaint: translatedComplaint,
    );
  }
}

// HOW TO SET THE KEY WITHOUT HARDCODING IT IN SOURCE (recommended even
// for a demo, so it doesn't end up in your public GitHub repo):
//
// Run / build with:
//   flutter run --dart-define=GROQ_API_KEY=your_key_here
//   flutter build apk --dart-define=GROQ_API_KEY=your_key_here
//
// The `String.fromEnvironment('GROQ_API_KEY', ...)` above will pick it up
// automatically — you don't need to touch the code above when doing this.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------

# FILE: lib\theme\app_theme.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AppTheme {
  static const Color primaryColor = Color(0xFF0D9488); // teal-600
  static const Color secondaryColor = Color(0xFF6366F1); // indigo-500
  static const Color backgroundColor = Color(0xFFF8FAFC); // slate-50

  static ThemeData get lightTheme {
    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: backgroundColor,
      colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
        seedColor: primaryColor,
        primary: primaryColor,
        secondary: secondaryColor,
        surface: Colors.white,
      ),
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        foregroundColor: Color(0xFF1E293B),
        elevation: 0,
        centerTitle: false,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 20,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
          color: Color(0xFF1E293B),
        ),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        elevation: 0,
        color: Colors.white,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          side: BorderSide(color: Colors.grey.shade200),
        ),
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: primaryColor,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 20),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
          elevation: 0,
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: primaryColor,
          side: BorderSide(color: primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14, horizontal: 18),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      textButtonTheme: TextButtonThemeData(
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: primaryColor,
          textStyle: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: const Color(0xFFF1F5F9),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 16),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: primaryColor, width: 1.6),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.redAccent, width: 1.2),
        ),
        labelStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade600),
      ),
      floatingActionButtonTheme: const FloatingActionButtonThemeData(
        backgroundColor: primaryColor,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
      ),
      textTheme: const TextTheme(
        headlineMedium: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Color(0xFF1E293B)),
        titleLarge: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: Color(0xFF1E293B)),
        bodyLarge: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF334155)),
        bodyMedium: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF64748B)),
      ),
    );
  }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: lib\utils\auth_error_translator.dart
################################################################################

// lib/utils/auth_error_translator.dart
//
// AuthService returns FirebaseAuthException.code (e.g. 'user-not-found')
// rather than e.message, because Firebase's e.message is always in
// English and can't be localized. This maps the common codes to a
// localized message; screens call this instead of showing the raw code.

import '../l10n/app_localizations.dart';

String authErrorMessage(AppLocalizations l10n, String? code, {required bool isSignup}) {
  switch (code) {
    case 'invalid-email':
      return l10n.authErrorInvalidEmail;
    case 'user-disabled':
      return l10n.authErrorUserDisabled;
    case 'user-not-found':
      return l10n.authErrorUserNotFound;
    case 'wrong-password':
      return l10n.authErrorWrongPassword;
    case 'invalid-credential':
      return l10n.authErrorInvalidCredential;
    case 'email-already-in-use':
      return l10n.authErrorEmailInUse;
    case 'weak-password':
      return l10n.authErrorWeakPassword;
    case 'network-request-failed':
      return l10n.authErrorNetwork;
    case 'too-many-requests':
      return l10n.authErrorTooManyRequests;
    default:
      return isSignup ? l10n.signupFailedGeneric : l10n.loginFailedGeneric;
  }
}

// This is a basic Flutter widget test.
//
// To perform an interaction with a widget in your test, use the WidgetTester
// utility in the flutter_test package. For example, you can send tap and scroll
// gestures. You can also use WidgetTester to find child widgets in the widget
// tree, read text, and verify that the values of widget properties are correct.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import 'package:flutterprojects/main.dart';

void main() {
  testWidgets('Counter increments smoke test', (WidgetTester tester) async {
    // Build our app and trigger a frame.
    await tester.pumpWidget(const MyApp());

    // Verify that our counter starts at 0.
    expect(find.text('0'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('1'), findsNothing);

    // Tap the '+' icon and trigger a frame.
    await tester.tap(find.byIcon(Icons.add));
    await tester.pump();

    // Verify that our counter has incremented.
    expect(find.text('0'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('1'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
